Conclusion: ψ(4160)质量上移并非必要，其历史偏差可能源于模型简化（如忽略多共振干涉与非淬火效应）；强调在强子谱分析中必须考虑共振间相干及完整谱系，而非孤立拟合单峰。

Abstract: The long-standing upward shift of the extracted $ψ(4160)$ mass, from about $4.16$~GeV to $4.19$~GeV in later analyses, remains a puzzling issue in charmonium spectroscopy. In our previous study, this problem was investigated through the $B^+\to K^+μ^+μ^-$ process within an unquenched charmonium framework, where the lower-mass $ψ(4160)$ assignment was found to be compatible with the data. Here we revisit the BESII $R$-value data, which played an important role in the historical extraction of the higher $ψ(4160)$ mass, and provide an independent examination. In contrast to the conventional quenched picture with $ψ(4040)$, $ψ(4160)$, and $ψ(4415)$, the unquenched vector-charmonium spectrum contains six states: $ψ(4040)$, $ψ(4160)$, $ψ(4220)$, $ψ(4380)$, $ψ(4415)$, and $ψ(4500)$. Including these states together with the near-threshold $ψ(3770)$, we find that the BESII $R$-value line shape can be well reproduced over the full energy range while retaining the lower-mass $ψ(4160)$ assignment. The enhancement around $4.19$~GeV then arises from the coherent interplay among the nearby $ψ(4040)$, $ψ(4160)$, and $ψ(4220)$ amplitudes, rather than requiring an upward shift of the $ψ(4160)$ mass itself. The additional higher states also naturally describe the line-shape structure in the $4.4$~GeV region. We further show that the seven-resonance coherent amplitude contains six complex zeros, yielding $2^6=64$ mathematically equivalent solutions with identical line shapes but substantially different di-electron widths and relative phases. Comparing these solutions with available experimental information and representative unquenched charmonium predictions, we provide a qualitative assessment of their phenomenological consistency and highlight several solutions that appear more compatible with present information.

</details>


### [100] [Neutralino Dark Matter in SU(5) after H.E.S.S. and Direct-Detection Searches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19675)
*M. Adeel Ajaib,Fariha Nasir*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在具有非通用胶子质量的超对称SU(5)模型中，伽马射线谱线与直接探测实验对中性微子暗物质的限制作用。结果表明，纯winos（~1 TeV）被H.E.S.S.谱线观测基本排除；higgsino和wino-higgsino情形被直接探测排除；而接近热产生丰度的bino情形则能同时避开两类实验限制。研究还探讨了不同银河晕密度分布、GUT尺度胶子质量层级及标量/Higgs边界条件的影响，并指出需结合电弱微子理论预测、谱依赖修正及对撞机检验以确定剩余参数空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解中性微子暗物质在超对称SU(5)大统一框架下的可检验性，特别是伽马射线谱线与地下直接探测实验对不同中性微子成分（bino/wino/higgsino）的互补约束能力。

Method: 基于超对称SU(5)模型，设定非通用胶子质量边界条件，结合银河晕 cusped/ cored 密度剖面，计算中性微子湮灭产生的伽马射线谱线通量（对比H.E.S.S.极限）及自旋无关散射截面（对比XENON/LUX等直接探测极限），系统扫描不同中性微子成分（纯bino、wino、higgsino及其混合）的参数空间。

Result: 纯wino（1 TeV）被H.E.S.S.谱线限制完全排除；higgsino和wino-higgsino被当前直接探测实验排除；bino主导且接近热丰度的解可同时满足两类实验上限；混合bino-wino解凸显两类探测手段的互补性及对晕剖面的依赖性；热不足解需额外产生产机制以满足全暗物质密度假设。

Conclusion: 伽马射线谱线与直接探测构成强互补探针，联合分析可显著压缩超对称SU(5)中中性微子暗物质的可行参数空间；未来需结合电弱微子理论预测的谱依赖修正及LHC等对撞机实验进一步验证幸存区域。

Abstract: We examine the impact of gamma-ray line and direct-detection searches on neutralino dark matter in supersymmetric SU(5) with non-universal gaugino masses. Under the assumption that neutralinos constitute all halo dark matter, a nearly pure wino benchmark at 1 TeV is essentially excluded by the H.E.S.S. line limits for both cusped and cored density profiles. Higgsino and wino-Higgsino benchmarks are independently excluded by direct detection. In contrast, a bino benchmark near thermal saturation combines suppressed present-day line emission with scattering below the displayed experimental limits. Mixed bino-wino solutions illustrate the complementarity of the two searches and the dependence of the line interpretation on the Galactic halo profile. We relate these results to the GUT-scale gaugino hierarchy and the SU(5) scalar and Higgs boundary conditions. Thermally underabundant solutions require additional production to realize the full-density assumption. Comparing the calculated signals with published electroweakino predictions, we identify the spectrum-dependent corrections and collider tests needed to determine the surviving parameter space.

</details>


### [101] [Hidden charm pentaquarks and the nature of $P_{c}$ states observed at LHCb](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19749)
*Zhi-Biao Liang,Jun-Jie Liu,Mu-Yang Chen,Xian-Hui Zhong*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在半相对论势模型框架下，统一研究了隐粲五夸克态（qqqc\bar{c}）的低能1S波紧致态与强子分子态，并用单玻色子交换势描述强子簇间相互作用，成功解释LHCb观测到的多个P_c态为Σ_c^{(*)}\bar{D}^{(*)}型分子态，指出σ/ρ介子交换对束缚态形成起关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释LHCb实验发现的多个隐粲五夸克态（如P_c(4312)^+, P_c(4440)^+, P_c(4457)^+等）的内部结构，区分紧致五夸克态与强子分子态，并探究其束缚机制。

Method: 采用半相对论势夸克模型，包含线性禁闭势、单胶子交换势以及强子簇间的单玻色子交换势（σ, ρ等）；结合夸克交换模型计算组分衰变，并分析不同同位旋和自旋-宇称组合下的束缚态谱。

Result: 成功将P_c(4312)^+, P_c(4440)^+, P_c(4457)^+分别解释为[Σ_c\bar{D}]_{1/2^-}^{1/2}、[Σ_c\bar{D}^*]_{1/2^-}^{1/2}、[Σ_c\bar{D}^*]_{3/2^-}^{1/2}分子态；P_c(4380)^+可能对应[Σ_c^*\bar{D}]_{3/2^-}^{1/2}，但理论预测宽度偏窄；σ和ρ交换对I=1/2的Σ_c^{(*)}\bar{D}^{(*)}束缚态至关重要；还预言可能存在I=1/2的Λ_c\bar{D}^{(*)}及I=3/2的Σ_c^{(*)}\bar{D}^{(*)}极浅束缚态。

Conclusion: LHCb观测到的多数P_c态更可能是由重子-介子组成的强子分子而非紧致五夸克态；σ/ρ介子交换是形成这些分子态的关键动力学机制；该工作为未来实验鉴别和寻找新型隐粲五夸克态提供了理论依据。

Abstract: We carry out a unified study of the low-lying $1S$-wave compact states and hadronic molecules composed of hidden charm pentaquarks $qqqc\bar{c}$ ($q=u,d$) within a semirelativistic potential quark model. Apart from the linear confinement and one-gluon exchange potential between quarks and/or antiquarks, one-boson exchange potential is also included for baryon and meson clusters within the pentaquark system. We also evaluate the fall-apart decays by combining the obtained spectra within the quark exchange model. It is found that the $P_c (4312)^+$, $P_c (4440)^+$, and $P_c (4457)^+$ observed by the LHCb Collaboration in 2019 can be well explained by the hadronic molecules of $[Σ_c\bar{D}]_{1/2^-}^{1/2}(4318)$, $[Σ_c\bar{D}^*]_{1/2^-}^{1/2}(4437)$, and $[Σ_c\bar{D}^*]_{3/2^-}^{1/2}(4458)$, respectively. Meanwhile, $P_c(4380)^+$ reported by LHCb in 2015 may be assigned as the molecule $[Σ_c^*\bar{D}]_{3/2^-}^{1/2}(4382)$, except that it turns to be a narrow state other than a broad one shown by the experimental data. Our study shows that the $σ$- and $ρ$-meson exchanges are crucial for the formation of $Σ_c^{(*)}\bar{D}^{(*)}$ bound states with isospin $I=1/2$. Depending on the potential strength of the $σ$ exchange, there may exist very shallow bound states of $Λ_c\bar{D}^{(*)}$ with isospin $I=1/2$ and $Σ_c^{(*)}\bar{D}^{(*)}$ with isospin $I=3/2$. Our study may provide useful information for further exploring the hidden-charm pentaquarks in future experiments.

</details>


### [102] [FLARE: an open-source data workflow orchestration tool](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19809)
*Cameron Cooper Harris,Aman Desai,Paul Jackson*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: Flare是一个基于Python的开源数据工作流编排工具，用于自动化未来环形对撞机（FCC）中蒙特卡洛事件生成、快速探测器模拟及物理分析的全流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为简化未来环形对撞机（FCC）特别是FCC-ee实验中复杂且定制化要求高的蒙特卡洛模拟与物理分析流程，需统一、自动化的工具支持。

Method: 基于b2luigi构建，集成Whizard、MadGraph5、Pythia8等MC生成器及Delphes快速探测器模拟，并耦合FCC物理分析软件（FCCAnalyses），通过Python API和定制化配置实现端到端工作流编排。

Result: 实现了从MC事件生成、探测器模拟到最终直方图输出的全自动流水线，显著提升FCC-ee分析效率与可重复性。

Conclusion: Flare作为Key4HEP生态的关键工具，增强了高能物理分析的自动化水平与用户友好性，尤其适用于需要定制MC样本的场景。

Abstract: Flare is an open-source Python-based data workflow orchestration tool powered by b2luigi. It automates the workflow of Monte Carlo (MC) generators inside the Key4HEP stack, such as Whizard, MadGraph5 and Pythia8, together with fast detector simulation using Delphes. It also automates the Future Circular Collider (FCC) physics analysis software workflow. These workflows are combined, giving a user an automated pipeline from MC production to final FCCAnalyses histograms. With its customisation options and Python API, Flare simplifies executing FCC-ee analyses, especially when custom MC events are required.

</details>


### [103] [Measure of the kaon structure: generalized valence quark distribution functions and form factors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19861)
*H. Nematollahi,K. Azizi*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用指数表示方案（ERS）和修正的手征夸克模型（χQM）计算了K+介子的价夸克广义部分子分布函数（GPDs）及其电磁与引力形状因子，并与实验及其他理论结果进行了比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究K+介子内部结构，特别是其价夸克的广义部分子分布函数（GPDs）和形状因子（FFs）。

Method: 采用基于指数表示方案（ERS）的理论框架，结合修正的手征夸克模型（χQM）获取K+介子价夸克分布函数，并在此基础上计算其GPDs及电磁、引力形状因子。

Result: 成功计算出K+介子的价夸克GPDs以及电磁和引力形状因子，并与现有实验数据和其他理论/唯象模型结果进行了对比分析。

Conclusion: 该方法为理解轻介子特别是K+介子的内部结构提供了有效途径，所获结果与实验及其他模型具有较好一致性。

Abstract: We investigate the structure of the $K^+$ meson through calculating its valence-quark (anti quark) generalized parton distribution functions (GPDs) and form factors (FFs). For this purpose, we use a theoretical framework which is based on the exponential representation scheme (ERS). In this scheme the valence-quark GPDs of the light mesons, including the kaon, are considered as the valence quark distribution function crossed by the exponential of a profile function, at zero skewness. We apply the modified chiral quark model ($χQM$ ) to obtain the input valence distribution functions of the kaon in above scheme and calculate its valence GPDs. The kaon's electromagnetic and gravitational form factors are also determined and compared with existing experimental data and the results of some theoretical and phenomenological models.

</details>


### [104] [Stasis Combs: Gravitational-Wave Signatures of Recurrent Cosmological Stasis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20280)
*Gabriela Barenboim,Anne-Katherine Burns*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文扩展了宇宙学静止（stasis）单周期谱模板至任意N次连续静止周期，提出一个可分解的N周期分段谱模板，并将其应用于三静止场景，验证了理论预测并讨论了多周期静止信号在引力波探测中的可检测性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为区分宇宙学常w模型与含多次静止的模型，需发展能刻画多周期静止特征的IGWB谱模板，并提升其作为强判据的能力。

Method: 推导N周期静止的闭式分段谱模板，分析振幅跃变与局域状态方程、平台谱倾角与历史累积倾斜的关系；应用该形式体系于三静止链式模型，检验有效性条件、数值验证平台比、分析相邻静止紧邻极限，并估算不同多周期场景的最小可探测张量标量比。

Result: 建立了N周期静止谱模板及其物理参数映射关系；确认Dienes等提出的图3基准点超出模板有效范围；给出可行且可探测的参数选择；数值验证了双/三静止链的平台比预测；导出了紧邻极限下的解析行为；给出了多周期静止所需的最小r值随N增加而降低的趋势。

Conclusion: 多周期静止在IGWB中产生可分辨的‘梳状’谱特征，每个分辨特征独立检验Bessel一致性关系，N个同时满足的概率随N急剧下降，因此实测多峰梳状结构将成为排除常w宇宙模型的最强证据。

Abstract: Cosmological stasis can recur multiple times, and each occurrence imprints a characteristic feature on the inflationary gravitational wave background (IGWB). We extend the single-epoch spectral template to an arbitrary number $N$ of consecutive stasis epochs, deriving a closed-form $N$-epoch piecewise template that factorizes cleanly: the amplitude steps at each break are determined entirely by the local equation of state $w_s^{(i)}$ of that epoch, while the inter-epoch plateau levels encode the accumulated spectral tilt from all prior epochs. Each resolved spectral feature yields an independent test of the Bessel-function consistency relation $C^2 = C^2(α)$ established in~\cite{BarenboimBurns:paper1}, and the probability that $N$ such tests are simultaneously satisfied by a non-stasis spectrum falls sharply with $N$, making a confirmed multi-epoch comb the strongest available discriminator against constant-$w$ alternatives.
  We apply the formalism to the triple-stasis scenario of~\cite{Dienes:2023ziv}, which chains three pairwise stasis mechanisms (matter/radiation, vacuum-energy/matter, vacuum-energy/radiation) sequentially. For each block we derive the conditions under which the epoch lies within the template's validity range $w_s > -1/3$. We show that the Fig.~3 benchmark of~\cite{Dienes:2023ziv} falls outside this range and identify parameter choices that are both physically realizable and accessible to the template. We validate the inter-plateau ratio predictions numerically for representative two- and three-block chains, and work out the touching-epoch limit in which consecutive blocks share a break frequency without an intervening radiation-dominated interval. Finally, we discuss the detectability of a variety of multi-epoch stasis scenarios and give the minimum detectable tensor-to-scalar ratio as a function of the number of recurrent stasis epochs.

</details>


### [105] [Colour sextet baryons in composite Higgs models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19918)
*Manuel Kunkel*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了复合希格斯模型中色六重态重子的唯象学，重点分析其衰变模式、产生机制及对撞机实验限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复合希格斯模型基于费米子底层结构，预言除顶夸克伙伴外还存在奇异色荷的重子（如色六重态），需系统研究其可观测信号与实验约束。

Method: 总结色六重态重子的衰变行为（主要通过色八重标量，产生多顶夸克末态；或在另类质量谱下产生底夸克与稳定单态）；利用现有对撞机数据进行再解释（recasting）以给出质量限制。

Result: 通过对LHC等实验数据的再分析，获得色六重态重子质量上限达2.64 TeV。

Conclusion: 色六重态重子是复合希格斯模型的重要可检验信号，其多顶夸克末态和特殊衰变拓扑为未来高亮度LHC提供了明确探测方向；当前实验已对其质量施加强约束。

Abstract: Composite Higgs models with underlying fermionic descriptions predict a rich spectrum beyond the Standard Model. Besides the colour triplet fermionic resonances that act as top partners, also exotically coloured baryons can appear. In this contribution we summarise the phenomenology of colour sextet baryons. The dominant decay channel proceeds via a colour octet scalar, resulting in pair production signatures with four or six top quarks. In an alternative mass hierarchy, the sextets produce bottom quarks and a collider-stable colour singlet. We derive recasting bounds on the sextet mass, which reach up to $2.64$ TeV.

</details>


### [106] [New massive resonances at the LHC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19919)
*Rosy Caliri*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了由部分复合性机制驱动的复合希格斯模型，其中希格斯玻色子是伪Nambu-Goldstone玻色子，顶夸克伙伴为三超费米子束缚态；重点分析了自旋-1共振态的LHC唯象学，预测存在两个中性与一个带电自旋-1共振态，其与标准模型规范玻色子显著混合，可在Drell-Yan过程中单产生，质量低至1.5 TeV仍与现有LHC数据兼容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索复合希格斯模型中自旋-1共振态的新物理现象及其在LHC上的可探测性，特别是其与标准模型规范玻色子的混合效应和低质量可行性。

Method: 基于具有SU(2)_L × SU(2)_R未破缺全局对称性的规范-费米子紫外完备模型，构建含部分复合性的复合希格斯框架；系统分析自旋-1共振态谱、混合结构及Drell-Yan单产生截面；结合当前LHC实验限制进行质量可行性评估。

Result: 预言存在两个中性与一个带电自旋-1共振态，显著混合于SM规范玻色子；其Drell-Yan单产生过程在LHC上可观测；质量可低至1.5 TeV且尚未被现有数据排除。

Conclusion: 该类复合希格斯模型中的自旋-1共振态是LHC上极具潜力的新物理探针，其低质量窗口仍开放，值得进一步实验搜寻与理论精细化研究。

Abstract: We investigate composite Higgs models arising from a gauge-fermionic UV completion with partial compositeness, where the Higgs boson emerges as a pseudo-Nambu-Goldstone boson (pNGB), and top-partners appear as bound states of three hyperfermions, with $\mathrm{SU}(2)_L \times \mathrm{SU}(2)_R$ as the unbroken global subgroup. A bunch of new bound states is predicted by composite Higgs models: spin-0, spin-1/2 and spin-1 resonances. In this work we focus on the phenomenology of the spin-1 resonances. We demonstrate that a generic prediction of these models is the existence of two neutral and one charged spin-1 resonances, which mix significantly with the SM gauge bosons. This mixing allows their single production in Drell-Yan processes at the LHC. We study the LHC phenomenology of these states and identify scenarios where their masses could be as low as $1.5~\mathrm{TeV}$, consistent with the existing LHC data.

</details>


### [107] [The axion mass in the post-inflationary scenario in a minimal scaling model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20801)
*José Correia,Mark Hindmarsh,Joanes Lizarraga,Asier Lopez-Eiguren,Kari Rummukainen,Jon Urrestilla*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过大规模经典场论模拟，计算了后暴胀场景下暗物质轴子的质量，得到其值为17.46(84) μeV，并讨论了非最小模型对轴子质量上限的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定后暴胀场景中轴子作为暗物质候选者的质量，需精确模拟弦-畴壁网络的动力学演化及其衰变为轴子辐射的过程，而此前结果存在较大不确定性。

Method: 在具有12288³网格的高分辨率经典场论模拟中，构建并演化一个最小Peccei-Quinn模型：U(1)对称性在高温下由复标量场破缺形成宇宙弦；QCD相变后生成畴壁，导致弦被拉近并湮灭；最终估算残留轴子辐射的合动量数密度，并结合格点QCD给出的拓扑磁化率确定轴子质量。

Result: 得到轴子质量为17.46(84) μeV，对应对称性破缺能标3.27(15)×10¹¹ GeV及卤探测器共振频率4.22(20) GHz；并指出含希格斯凝聚或规范通量的非最小模型预测轴子质量一般低于约40 μeV。

Conclusion: 该高精度模拟显著提升了后暴胀轴子质量预测的可靠性，支持轴子在μeV量级作为冷暗物质的可行性，并为未来卤探测实验提供了关键参数依据。

Abstract: We calculate the dark matter axion mass in the post-inflationary scenario in a minimal model with a set of classical field theory simulations on $12\,288^3$ grids. In the model, the Peccei-Quinn U(1) symmetry is broken by a complex scalar field at a temperature $f_{\rm a} \sim 10^{11}$ GeV, creating a string network. The strings become the boundaries of domain walls at the QCD transition, which draw the strings together and annihilate them. The decay of the strings and domain walls leaves behind axion radiation, with a comoving number density estimated to be $9.48(23)f_{\rm a}^2 H_*$, normalised to unit scale factor when the axion mass is equal to the Hubble rate $H_*$. Using the most commonly quoted lattice results for the topological susceptibility, the dark matter axion mass in this minimal model is $17.46(84)\,μ\text{eV}$. This corresponds to a symmetry-breaking scale of $3.27(15) \times 10^{11}\, \text{GeV}$, and a haloscope resonant frequency of $4.22(20)\,\text{GHz}$. We argue that field theory simulations of non-minimal models where the strings have a Higgs condensate or gauge flux, and are annihilated by domain walls, will give axion mass predictions below about $40\,μ\text{eV}$.

</details>


### [108] [Study of the $e^+e^- \to φK^+K^-$ reaction within triangle dynamics and its implications for the $φ(2170)$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19951)
*Xiang Wei,Cheng Chen,Si-Wei Liu,Zu-Xin Cai,Gang Li,Xiao-Hai Liu,Ju-Jun Xie*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文重新研究了e⁺e⁻ → φπ⁺π⁻反应，并在K₁-K̄-K三角动力学框架下解释了φ(2170)态的产生机制；同时预测并验证了e⁺e⁻ → φK⁺K⁻反应的截面，发现其无明显φ(2170)信号，源于三角奇点被强烈压制及树图与圈图振幅干涉相消。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释e⁺e⁻ → φπ⁺π⁻中观测到的φ(2170)共振态起源，并检验该态是否也在e⁺e⁻ → φK⁺K⁻中出现，从而验证K₁-K̄-K三角机制的普适性。

Method: 采用K₁-K̄-K三角动力学模型：e⁺e⁻通过单光子交换产生K₁K̄对，K₁衰变至φK，K̄K末态发生π⁺π⁻重散射；统一参数下同时计算φπ⁺π⁻和φK⁺K⁻两个通道，并分析三角奇点位置、相空间限制及树图-圈图干涉效应。

Result: e⁺e⁻ → φK⁺K⁻的理论总截面与BESIII实验数据吻合良好；φK⁺K⁻通道中三角奇点被显著压制（因K⁺K⁻阈值升高、相空间受限），且树图与圈图干涉导致剩余信号消失，因而未观测到明显φ(2170)信号。

Conclusion: φ(2170)在e⁺e⁻ → φπ⁺π⁻中由K₁-K̄-K三角环产生，该机制同样适用于φK⁺K⁻通道；其在后者中缺失是动力学自然结果，为模型提供了强有力验证，并支持两反应共享同一物理起源。

Abstract: We revisit the $e^+e^- \to φπ^+π^-$ reaction within the $K_1$-$\bar{K}$-$K$ triangle dynamics framework, in which the $e^+e^-$ pair annihilates through one-photon exchange approximation to produce a $K_1\bar{K}$ pair, followed by the $K_1 \toφK$ decay and the final-state $K\bar{K} \to π^+π^-$ rescattering. With the same theoretical formalism and model parameters, the $e^+e^- \to φK^+K^-$ reaction is investigated, and it is found that the predicted total cross sections for the $e^+ e^- \to φK^+ K^-$ reaction are in good agreement with the existing BESIII measurements. Our study shows that the triangle singularity in the $φK^+K^-$ channel is strongly suppressed, because the higher $K^+K^-$ mass threshold shifts the kinematics away from the triangle singularity condition and the phase space near the $K_1\bar{K}$ threshold is very limited. Moreover, the interference between the tree-level and loop amplitudes eliminates the remaining signal. These combined effects naturally explain the absence of a distinct $φ(2170)$ signal in the $e^+ e^- \to φK^+ K^-$ reaction, provide a strong test of the model, and reinforce the picture that both reactions are governed by the same underlying mechanism, in which the $φ(2170)$ state is produced in the $e^+ e^-$ annihilation from the $K_1$-$\bar{K}$-$K$ triangle loop.

</details>


### [109] [Paired charmonium and bottomonium production in rare exclusive decays of Z boson](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19952)
*F. A. Martynenko,A. P. Martynenko,A. A. Skakun*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在相对论夸克模型框架下研究了Z玻色子衰变中成对的粲偶素和底偶素产生过程，计算了不同衰变机制下的衰变宽度，并考虑了非相对论近似及相对论修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解Z玻色子衰变中重夸克偶素（如粲偶素和底偶素）的成对产生机制，检验强相互作用理论在高能过程中的适用性。

Method: 采用相对论夸克模型，构建考虑重夸克相对动量的衰变振幅，并分别在非相对论近似和加入相对论修正下计算衰变宽度。

Result: 得到了不同衰变机制下成对粲偶素和底偶素产生的衰变宽度，量化了相对论修正的影响。

Conclusion: 相对论效应在Z玻色子衰变成对重偶素过程中不可忽略，该研究为实验探测和理论建模提供了重要参考。

Abstract: The processes of paired charmonium and bottomonium production in Z-boson decays are investigated within the relativistic quark model. Various decay mechanisms are examined, and relativistic decay amplitudes are constructed, taking into account the relative momenta of heavy quarks. Decay widths are calculated for various mechanisms in the nonrelativistic approximation and taking into account relativistic corrections.

</details>


### [110] [On the Maximal CP Violation in Leptogenesis with Two Right Handed Neutrinos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20033)
*Swapnil Dutta*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文推导了在双右手中微子（RHNs）最小I型跷跷板模型中，标准轻子生成（leptogenesis）下CP破坏衰变不对称性的严格解析上限，该上限是有效中微子质量的函数，并发现其比以往结果更紧，尤其在倒置质量序下偏离Davidson-Ibarra界达两个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为获得双RHN情形下更精确、更严格的CP不对称性上限，以完善轻子生成理论约束，并与三RHN标准情形（Davidson-Ibarra界）进行比较。

Method: 通过解析求解由轻子生成条件导出的四次方程根，建立CP不对称性、有效中微子质量等物理量之间的精确关系，在分层极限下推导出CP不对称性的解析上限。

Result: 得到CP不对称性上限是有效中微子质量的单调增函数，大质量区趋于全局最大值；该上限严于以往解析结果；双RHN全局最大CP不对称性低于三RHN的Davidson-Ibarra界，尤其在倒置质量序下偏差近两个数量级。

Conclusion: 双RHN轻子生成模型对最轻RHN质量施加更严格下限，且倒置质量序下理论约束显著增强，支持此前文献中关于双RHN情形更受限的结论。

Abstract: We present a generalized exact analytical expression for the upper bound on the CP-violating decay asymmetry in the case of standard vanilla leptogenesis within the minimal type-I seesaw with two Right Handed Neutrinos (RHNs) case scenario as a function of the effective neutrino mass in the hierarchical limit. We derive the bound using a novel analytical treatment of the roots of a quartic equation arising from the conditions for successful leptogenesis, in which the CP asymmetry, effective neutrino mass, and other physical quantities enter as parameters. We find that the upper bound on CP asymmetry is a monotonically increasing function of the effective neutrino mass, asymptotically saturating to a global maximum at the large effective mass regime. We find that our exact analytical expression of the upper bound as a function of the effective neutrino mass is smaller than the previous analytical results in the literature. We also find that the global maximum on CP asymmetry with two RHNs obtained at the large effective mass regime is less than the Davidson-Ibarra bound corresponding to the standard vanilla leptogenesis scenario with three RHNs, leading to a more stringent bound on the lightest RHN mass for the scenario with two RHNs, as expected from some previous results in literature. We find that the deviation from the standard Davidson-Ibarra bound becomes most dramatic for inverted neutrino mass hierarchy, by nearly two orders of magnitude.

</details>


### [111] [Comparing theoretical descriptions of Drell-Yan angular coefficients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20362)
*Nikita Gramotkov,Oleg Teryaev*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文对Drell-Yan过程中Z玻色子产生的角系数的三种理论描述（TMD、pQCD和几何模型）进行了定量比较，并结合ATLAS、CMS和LHCb的最新实验数据，发现TMD在中心快度区低横动量下对A0和A2系数描述最佳，且A0、A2及Lam–Tung关系是最灵敏的观测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了厘清不同理论框架（TMD、pQCD、几何模型）在Drell-Yan过程中对角系数描述的适用范围与精度，需借助最新高精度实验数据进行系统性定量检验。

Method: 对三种理论模型（TMD、pQCD、几何模型）预测的Drell-Yan Z玻色子角系数（A0–A4）进行定量计算，并与ATLAS、CMS和LHCb在中心快度区低横动量（low-QT）下的新实验数据对比分析。

Result: TMD模型在低-QT中心快度区对A0和A2系数的描述最优；A1、A3、A4差异较小；A0、A2及Lam–Tung关系被确认为最敏感观测量，并明确了各模型的适用区域。

Conclusion: TMD框架在低横动量区域更具优势，尤其适用于A0和A2角系数及Lam–Tung关系的研究；三种理论各有适用边界，需依据具体观测量和运动学区域选择合适模型。

Abstract: A quantitative comparison of the predictions of three theoretical descriptions (TMD, pQCD and geometrical) of the angular coefficients in Drell-Yan Z-boson production with new experimental data on angular coefficients obtained by the ATLAS, CMS and LHCb collaborations has been performed. In the central rapidity region at low-$Q_T$, the TMD calculation provides a better description of the $A_0$ and $A_2$ coefficients among the descriptions considered. The differences between the approaches are less pronounced for $A_1$,$A_3$ and $A_4$ coefficients. The results identify $A_0$,$A_2$ and the Lam--Tung relation as the most sensitive observables at low-QT and illustrate the domains of applicability of the three descriptions.

</details>


### [112] [Investigation of low-lying $Ω_b(1P)$ states in an unquenched coupled-channel framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20392)
*Zi-Le Zhang,Si-Qiang Luo,Shuai-Wei Wang,Qin Chang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了非淬火效应对低能Ω_b(1P)态的影响，发现J^P=1/2^-态因S波耦合到Ξ_bK̄通道而表现出显著的耦合道效应，可能导致其质量接近或低于该阈值，从而开启辐射和同位旋破缺衰变道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示非淬火效应如何影响Ω_b(1P)能谱与组分混合，为实验探测激发底重子提供理论指导。

Method: 采用耦合道方程方法研究Ω_b(1P)态的非淬火效应。

Result: J^P=1/2^-的Ω_b(1P)态（以j_ℓ=0组态为主）因S波耦合到Ξ_bK̄通道而产生显著耦合道效应，其质量可能被推至或低于该阈值，使辐射与同位旋破缺衰变在运动学上允许。

Conclusion: 本工作从耦合道角度提供了对低能Ω_b(1P)谱的新认识，并为未来实验研究激发底重子提供了重要启示。

Abstract: In this work, we study the unquenched effects on the low-lying $Ω_b(1P)$ states with a coupled-channel equations. We reveal how the unquenched effects affect the $Ω_b(1P)$ spectroscopy and component mixing. The numerical results indicate that the $J^P=1/2^-$ $Ω_b(1P)$ state dominated by the $j_\ell=0$ configuration exhibits significant coupled-channel effects due to its $S$-wave coupling to the $Ξ_b\bar{K}$ channel, where $j_\ell$ denotes the total angular momentum of the light flavor degrees-of-freedom. In this scenario, the mass of this state may be shifted close to or below the $Ξ_b\bar{K}$ threshold, making the radiative and isospin-breaking channels kinematically allowed decay processes. The present analysis provides a coupled-channel perspective on the low-lying $Ω_b(1P)$ spectrum and offers guidance for future experimental studies of excited bottom baryons.

</details>


### [113] [A quantum representation of $π$ fragmentation functions through variational quantum circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20557)
*David F. Rentería-Estrada,Roger J. Hernández-Pinto,Germán Rodrigo,Rodolfo Sassot,German F. R. Sborlini*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于变分量子电路的碎裂函数（FFs）建模方法，利用对称性约束、物理启发的参数化设计和量子纠缠，实现了高精度、低冗余、易优化的非微扰参数化，并成功扩展到能量尺度依赖的二维表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统碎裂函数建模面临非微扰强子化过程难以精确描述、参数冗余、优化困难等问题；需发展兼具物理合理性与计算高效性的新方法。

Method: 构建满足同位旋和电荷共轭对称性的六味独立基底；引入对数特征映射与质量阈值等物理启发Ansätze；设计一维（动量分数）和二维（动量分数+能量尺度）变分量子电路（FF-VQR），利用量子纠缠耦合夸克与胶子FF通道。

Result: 一维FF-VQR仅用两层变分电路即可高精度复现DSS14基准结果；谱分析表明其以少量傅里叶模实现高表达力；二维扩展通过单一体纠缠电路统一编码所有味通道，精度优于各味独立编码。

Conclusion: 该量子电路模型为碎裂函数提供了紧凑、物理驱动且可进行DGLAP演化的非微扰参数化新范式，展示了量子机器学习在高能核物理建模中的实际潜力。

Abstract: We present a variational quantum-circuit model for fragmentation functions (FFs). Isospin and charge-conjugation symmetries are imposed to construct an independent six-flavor basis describing charged and neutral pion production, while physics-inspired Ansätze, including logarithmic feature maps and mass thresholds, encode the relevant kinematics. This quantum architecture substantially reduces the quantum circuit redundancies and improve optimization convergence. Using the DSS14 pion FF set as a benchmark, we first develop a one-dimensional variational representation (FF-VQR) in the momentum fraction at fixed energy scale, and show how entanglement between quark and gluon FFs yields a significant improvement, with accurate results already obtained using just two variational layers. A spectral analysis further demonstrates that the quantum model achieves high expressivity with a limited number of Fourier modes, supporting its use as a compact non-perturbative parametrization suitable for DGLAP evolution. We then extend the FF-VQR to two dimensions by incorporating the energy-scale dependence. By encoding all flavor channels within a single entangled quantum circuit, the quantum model provides a unified representation with higher accuracy than an independent encoding for each partonic species.

</details>


### [114] [Line shapes of $Ω(2012)$ production in the $Ξ\bar K $ and $Ξπ\bar K$ decay channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20654)
*Natsumi Ikeno,Eulogio Oset*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文从理论上研究了Ω(2012)粒子在Ξ⁻π⁺K⁻和Ξ⁰K⁻衰变道中的线形，支持其为由Ξ* K̄ 和 Ωη 耦合通道动力学生成的分子态，并指出直接比较实验质量分布比以往使用的宽度比R更严格地检验该分子图像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验Ω(2012)是否为Ξ*K̄-Ωη-ΞK̄耦合通道动力学产生的分子态，并提供比现有宽度比R更严格的检验方法。

Method: 基于耦合通道理论，分析Ω(2012)在Ξ⁻π⁺K⁻和Ξ⁰K⁻衰变道中的线形，考虑Ξ*K̄、Ωη和ΞK̄通道，并考察πΞ不变质量截断对结果的影响。

Result: 理论线形与实验质量分布一致，支持Ω(2012)的分子态解释；发现结果对πΞ不变质量截断敏感；提出新方法计算三体与两体衰变宽度比R，以更好检验分子图像。

Conclusion: Ω(2012)很可能是由Ξ*K̄和Ωη相互作用形成的分子态；直接拟合实验质量分布比仅依赖R值更能严格验证其分子本质。

Abstract: We theoretically study the line shape of the experiment for $Ω(2012)$ production in the $Ξ^- π^+ K^-$ and $Ξ^0 K^-$ decay modes, reflecting the $Ξ^* \bar K$ and $Ξ\bar K$ decay modes, from the perspective that the $Ω(2012)$ is a molecular state dynamically generated from the interaction of the $Ξ^* \bar K $ and $Ωη$, coupled channels with $Ξ\bar K$ as a decay channel. We show the consistency of the picture with the experimental mass distributions, giving support to the molecular picture for the $Ω(2012)$ state. We also call the attention to the sensitivity of the results to the cut imposed on the $πΞ$ invariant mass, and suggest a different method to obtain the ratio $R^{Ξπ\bar K}_{Ξ\bar K}$ of the three-body to two-body decay widths used so far as a test of the molecular picture. We stress that the direct comparison with the experimental mass distributions done here is a more stringent test of the molecular nature of the $Ω(2012)$ state than the comparison of the ratio $R^{Ξπ\bar K}_{Ξ\bar K}$ which has been so far obtained using different criteria.

</details>


### [115] [Strongly Coupled Soft Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10083)
*Bruno Scheihing-Hitschfeld,Zhiquan Sun*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用AdS/CFT对偶，在强耦合下计算了N=4超杨-米尔斯理论中带尖点的Wilson圈的真空期望值，重点研究了单尖点和双尖点Wilson圈构型，得到了尖点反常维数Γ_cusp[Δη, Δθ]，并讨论了其在重夸克横动量依赖碎裂及TMD软函数中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尖点反常维数是QCD中许多过程中共有的普适量，源于胶子相互作用导致的发散；本文旨在通过强耦合下的全息方法补充微扰计算，研究横向分离的双尖点Wilson线构型及其在TMD软函数等物理可观测量中的联系。

Method: 基于AdS/CFT对应，采用Minkowski度规下的Nambu-Goto作用量分析，系统研究单尖点Wilson线构型的鞍点解（发现两类主导不同参数区间的鞍点），并推广至带横向分离的双尖点Wilson圈；在大快度极限下确定极值面的横向坐标轮廓。

Result: 完整计算了尖点反常维数Γ_cusp[Δη, Δθ]在整个Δη范围内的行为，特别给出Δθ=0和Δθ=π的结果，并与文献已有结果比对；获得了双尖点Wilson圈在横向分离下的真空期望值及其极值面几何结构。

Conclusion: 全息方法可有效刻画强耦合下尖点相关发散结构；所得结果为理解TMD软函数及其他具有全息对偶的规范理论中的软辐射提供新视角，并支持将该框架推广至更一般情形。

Abstract: The renormalization of operators built out of Wilson lines that meet at an angle (cusp) involve what is known as the cusp anomalous dimension, a universal object appearing in many processes in QCD due to the divergences from gluonic interactions. In this paper, we consider the vacuum expectation value of a pair of cusped Wilson lines separated in the transverse direction. This configuration can be related to a simple observable in heavy quark transverse momentum-dependent (TMD) fragmentation as well as the TMD soft function. We compute the expectation values of Wilson loops in $N=4$ super Yang-Mills theory at strong coupling via the AdS/CFT correspondence, an approach complementary to perturbative calculations of the cusp anomalous dimension. We first present a thorough analysis, directly in Minkowski signature, of the Nambu-Goto action and its saddle points for a Wilson line configuration with a single cusp. We find that there are two different classes of saddle points whose contributions dominate different regions of parameter space. We calculate the cusp anomalous dimension $Γ_{\rm cusp}[Δη, Δθ]$ for the whole range of $Δη$ from this setup, focusing on the cases $Δθ= 0$ and $Δθ= π$, and compare it with previous results in literature. We then use the techniques we developed to compute the expectation value of the two-cusp Wilson loop with transverse separation, and determine the profile of the transverse coordinate on the extremal surface in the large rapidity limit. We discuss the possible generalizations of our setup and results to soft functions in other gauge theories with a holographic dual.

</details>


### [116] [Adiabatic hydrodynamization with transverse spatial gradients in boost-invariant plasmas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20705)
*Uri Sharell,Jasmine Brewer,Weiyao Ke*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了远离平衡态的QCD物质如何快速进入流体动力学行为，通过引入有限横向波数k的梯度模，扩展了先前的均匀性假设分析，发现系统在特定条件下会趋向由声波和剪切模式张成的整体吸引子流形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相对论粘性流体力学在描述短寿命夸克-胶子等离子体方面取得了定量成功，但其快速达到流体行为的根本机制尚不清楚。

Method: 通过引入具有有限横向波数k的梯度模，放松横向均匀性假设，并结合动理学理论分析不同球谐分量间的耦合及其与膨胀率、碰撞率和梯度尺度的竞争关系。

Result: 发现在早期纵向膨胀抑制梯度耦合，各球谐分量遵循其均匀吸引子；后期小k下梯度耦合驱动系统趋向由声波和剪切模式构成的全局吸引子流形，特征时间τ_D依赖于方位角谐波m和kτ_R；当kτ_R足够大时谱隙关闭，扰动保持有限阻尼率但不再遵循孤立流体吸引子。

Conclusion: 梯度效应显著影响非平衡QCD物质向流体行为的演化路径，存在一个由kτ_R控制的过渡区域，在此区域内是否能用少数流体模式有效描述系统发生根本转变。

Abstract: The quantitative success of relativistic viscous hydrodynamics in describing the short-lived quark--gluon plasma raises a fundamental question: how does far-from-equilibrium QCD matter approach hydrodynamic behavior so rapidly? Microscopic kinetic-theory studies have related this onset to attractor dynamics, but typically assume transverse homogeneity. We relax this assumption by introducing gradient modes with finite transverse wave number $k$, extending the analysis of arXiv:2212.00820. These couple different spherical harmonics of the momentum distribution, and the resulting dynamics is controlled by the competition among the expansion rate $1/τ$, the collision rate $1/τ_R$, and the gradient scale $k$. At early times, longitudinal expansion suppresses this coupling, and different spherical harmonic sectors follow their homogeneous attractors. We find that, at later times and sufficiently small $k$, this gradient coupling drives the system toward a global attractor manifold spanned by hydrodynamic sound and shear modes, on a timescale $τ_D$ that depends on both the azimuthal harmonic $m$ and $kτ_R$. For sufficiently large $kτ_R$, the spectral gap closes: perturbations retain finite damping rates and therefore equilibrate, but no longer follow an isolated hydrodynamic attractor or admit a reduced description involving only a few hydrodynamic modes.

</details>


### [117] [An invertible map between 3D Breit-frame mechanical distributions and 2D infinite-momentum-frame mechanical densities in spin-1 hadrons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20714)
*Kemal Tezgin*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文构建了3D Breit帧与2D无穷动量帧下自旋-1强子的压强和剪切力分布之间的可逆映射，通过结合几何Abel变换与反变换及反冲算符，并分离Wigner旋转引起的混合效应，揭示了不同参考系下力学分布的对应关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立不同参考系（Breit帧与无穷动量帧）下自旋-1强子力学分布（压强与剪切力）之间的严格对应关系，以统一理解其内部机械结构。

Method: 结合几何前向与逆Abel变换、反冲算符，并分离Wigner旋转导致的无穷动量帧侧混合效应；针对自旋-1情形处理额外的多极结构及其对力学密度的约束传播。

Result: 实现了3D Breit帧压力/剪切力分布与2D无穷动量帧压力/剪切力密度之间的可逆映射；证明额外多极结构不引入新力学信息，仅受形式因子约束所支配。

Conclusion: 尽管自旋-1强子中Wigner旋转引起更复杂的坐标空间关系，但其力学分布的本质信息仍由受限的形式因子完全决定，不同参考系下的描述是等价且互补的。

Abstract: We construct an invertible map between the complete set of 3D Breit-frame pressure and shear force distributions and the constrained space of 2D infinite-momentum-frame pressure and shear force densities in coordinate space for spin-1 hadrons. The correspondence between the two sets of distributions in different frames is established by combining the geometric forward and inverse Abel transformations with recoil operators after separating the Wigner rotation induced mixing effects on the infinite-momentum-frame side. Unlike in the spin-1/2 case, these mixings complicate the coordinate space relations for spin-1 hadrons by introducing additional multipole structures, but the constraints among the form factors propagate into constraints on mechanical densities, so that these additional multipoles do not introduce new mechanical information.

</details>


### [118] [Electromagnetic form factors of vector mesons in Einstein-dilaton holographic QCD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20736)
*Alfonso Ballon-Bayona,Tobias Frederico,Luis A. H. Mamani,Adão S. da Silva Junior,Wayne de Paula*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在基于Einstein-dilaton引力的全息QCD框架下研究ρ介子族的电磁形状因子，结合谱分解与KK展开两种方法计算弹性及跃迁形状因子，并建立电流矩阵元的全息字典；结果与格点QCD弹性数据一致，给出尚未被格点计算的跃迁形状因子预测，并导出新的超收敛关系与求和规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解强子电磁结构，尤其是ρ介子族的弹性与跃迁形状因子，弥补格点QCD在跃迁过程中的缺失，并探索全息QCD对高能行为与守恒律（如超收敛关系）的描述能力。

Method: 采用基于Einstein-dilaton引力的全息QCD模型，在禁闭背景中通过三点同位旋流关联函数的谱分解和Kaluza-Klein展开两种独立方法计算电磁形状因子；构建电流矩阵元的全息字典；提取电荷半径、磁矩、四极矩及渐近归一化等可观测量。

Result: 弹性形状因子与格点QCD一致；发现ρ介子电形状因子在q²=6m_ρⁿ²处有零点（基态为3.61 GeV²），与光前和Dyson-Schwinger结果相符；给出跃迁形状因子的理论预测；导出高q²下的标度行为与渐近归一化；建立含质量、衰变常数和耦合常数的新求和规则。

Conclusion: 该全息模型能统一描述ρ介子族从低能到高能的电磁结构，验证了其对强子谱、形状因子及守恒律的自洽性，为非微扰QCD提供有力约束与预言工具。

Abstract: We investigate the electromagnetic form factors of the $ρ$ meson family within a holographic QCD framework based on Einstein-dilaton gravity with confining backgrounds. We obtain the elastic and transition form factors using a spectral decomposition of the three-point isospin current correlator and an independent approach based on the Kaluza-Klein expansion. Furthermore, we establish a holographic dictionary of the current matrix element. The elastic form factors are in agreement with available lattice QCD results, whereas the transition form factors, not yet determined within lattice QCD, are predictions of the model. For the elastic case, the electric form factor for the $ρ$ meson family exhibits a zero at $q^{2} = 6\, m_{ρ^{n}}^{2}$ and in particular for the ground state $q^{2} = 3.61\,\text{GeV}^{2}$, consistent with light-front and Dyson-Schwinger calculations. We also compute strong couplings between vector meson states and find some approximate selection rules. In the elastic case and low-$q^{2}$ regime, we calculate dimensionless electric radius, magnetic and quadrupole moments and compare with other approaches. In the high-$q^{2}$ regime, the form factors follow the expected scaling behavior, and the model predicts their asymptotic normalization. The results in the high-$q^{2}$ regime lead to superconvergence relations and establish a new sum rule involving masses, decay constants and couplings.

</details>

### [119] [Where the Forest Goes Dark: Halo-centered Characterization of High Column Density Systems with IllustrisTNG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19235)
*Victor R. Soares da Silva,Daniel López-Cano,L. Raul Abramo,J. Chaves-Montero,Francisco Maion*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文利用TNG50流体动力学模拟，系统识别并分离高柱密度中性氢结构（HCDs），分析其在不同冲击参数和晕质量下的覆盖分数，揭示了中性气体分布以维里半径为标度、几乎与质量无关的规律，并指出单Voigt组分可准确拟合Damped系统，但不适用于Lyman Limit Systems（LLSs）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高柱密度中性氢系统（HCDs）会污染Lyα森林的3D和1D统计，影响宇宙学约束精度；现有数据处理难以完全剔除弱LLS，需通过模拟深入理解其起源与特征。

Method: 基于TNG50模拟的气体网格识别HCD结构，在速度空间去混淆，将每条视线分为仅含HCD和仅含Lyα森林的光谱；统计z≈3时不同晕质量下、至50倍维里半径内各类型吸收（DLA/sub-DLA/LLS/forest）随冲击参数b的覆盖分数。

Result: 发现吸收类型随b/R200c转变：DLA→sub-DLA（≈0.11 R200c）、sub-DLA→LLS（≈0.3）、LLS→Lyα森林（≈0.5），后者在R200c处覆盖约78%视线；该序列在三个数量级晕质量范围内近似质量无关；单Voigt组分可精确拟合Damped系统柱密度，但不适用于LLS（常由多结构叠加贡献）。

Conclusion: 维里半径是中性气体空间分布的关键标度；本文建立的HCD详细表征为未来直接前向建模其对Lyα光谱的贡献奠定了基础。

Abstract: High column density systems of neutral hydrogen (HCDs) --- Lyman limit systems (LLSs), sub-damped and damped Ly$α$ absorbers (DLAs) --- contaminate both the 3D and the 1D statistics of the Ly$α$ forest. Most DLAs are masked out from the analyses, but cleaning algorithms are not perfect, and weaker LLSs are individually undetectable, so the residual contamination is absorbed into nuisance parameters that dilute the cosmological constraining power. Simulations provide the ideal setting to study this effect, allowing absorption features to be directly traced back to the gas producing them. We identify high-column-density structures on the gas cells of the TNG50 hydrodynamical simulation, deblend them in velocity space, and split every sightline into HCD-only and forest-only spectra. Applied to halos at $z \simeq 3$ out to 50 virial radii, it yields each class's covering fraction against impact parameter $b$ and halo mass. The impact parameter defines the type of absorption appearing in the spectrum: DLAs give way to sub-DLAs at $b \approx 0.11\,R_{200c}$, sub-DLAs to LLSs at $0.3$, and LLSs to the Ly$α$-forest at $0.5$, which covers $\simeq 78\%$ of sightlines at $R_{200c}$. In units of $R_{200c}$ the sequence is nearly mass-independent over three decades in mass, so the virial radius sets the scale of the neutral gas distribution. A single Voigt component recovers the column density of essentially every damped system, but not of LLSs, whose absorption features often arise from several separate contributions from gas structures along the sightline. Our detailed characterization of HCDs gives the first step to the construction of advanced techniques to directly forward-model their contribution to Ly$α$ spectra.

</details>


### [120] [On the Clustering Bias of Unresolved Gamma-Ray Sources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19259)
*Bhashin A. Thakore,Marco Regis,Michela Negro,Stefano Camera,Daniel Gruen,Nicolao Fornengo,Aaron J. Roodman*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文通过弱透镜-未解析γ射线背景（UGRB）交叉相关分析，结合参考耀变体模型，推断出未解析耀变体群体的平均线性偏置和宿主晕质量，并发现结果主要由UGRB的聚类特性决定，而非γ射线源模型细节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用UGRB与大尺度结构示踪器的交叉相关来表征太暗而无法单独探测的源群体。

Method: 采用参考耀变体模型，建模γ射线光度与宿主晕质量的关系，推断未解析耀变体群体的平均线性偏置⟨b(z)⟩和宿主晕质量⟨M(z)⟩；并用错位活动星系核（misaligned-AGN）模型进行对比验证。

Result: 信号主要来自中等偏置源（z≈1时⟨b⟩≳2），宿主晕质量约为𝒪(10¹³ M⊙)；错位AGN模型给出一致的偏置值。

Conclusion: UGRB的交叉相关信号主要反映其固有的聚类性质，而非特定γ射线源物理模型的细节。

Abstract: The Unresolved $γ$-Ray Background (UGRB) encodes the collective emission of source populations that are too faint to be detected individually, and its cross-correlation with tracers of the large-scale structure has emerged as a powerful tool to characterize those populations. In this work, we analyze the weak-lensing--UGRB cross-correlation using a reference blazar model. By modeling the relation between blazar $γ$-ray luminosity and host-halo mass, we infer the average linear bias $\langle b(z) \rangle$ and host-halo mass $\langle M(z) \rangle$ of the unresolved blazar population. We find that the signal is dominated by moderately biased sources, with $\langle b \rangle \gtrsim 2$ at $z\simeq 1$, hosted by halos with $\langle M \rangle \sim \mathcal{O}(10^{13}\,M_\odot)$. Repeating the analysis with a misaligned-AGN model yields consistent bias values, indicating that the result is determined by the clustering properties of the UGRB rather than by the details of the $γ$-ray source model.

</details>


### [121] [Generalised Visibility ILC: a new foreground mitigation strategy for interferometric HI intensity mapping in the low-redshift Universe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19276)
*M. Ruiz-Granda,A. Mazumder,L. Wolz,M. Remazeilles,D. Herranz,P. Vielva*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的非参数前景清理方法GVILC，结合PCA与多维ILC，在可见度空间中有效抑制前景污染并恢复中性氢（HI）信号，显著优于仅使用前景规避策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中性氢（HI）强度映射是研究大尺度物质分布及宇宙学的重要工具，但其应用受限于难以从强得多的前景辐射中分离出微弱的HI信号。

Method: 提出广义可见度内线性组合（GVILC）方法：利用先验HI信号+噪声协方差矩阵指导的主成分分析（PCA）识别前景子空间，并在可见度空间中实施多维内部线性组合（ILC）；PCA去模数由数据驱动统计方法确定，并结合前景规避策略。

Result: GVILC在MeerKAT和SKA-Mid类模拟中显著压制前景残余，HI信号损失小；与前景规避联用可进一步防止信号损失并恢复原被污染的模式；均方误差显示其性能优于仅前景规避；引入更真实的HI模型（含谱线展宽与HI-连续谱源空间关联）会增加信号损失，但不改变GVILC所选模数。

Conclusion: GVILC是一种有效处理射电干涉数据中HI强度映射前景污染的方法，能恢复以往不可达傅里叶空间区域的宇宙学信息。

Abstract: HI intensity mapping is a powerful probe of the large-scale matter distribution and of the underlying astrophysics and cosmology. However, its application is limited by the challenge of separating the faint neutral hydrogen (HI) signal from much brighter foreground emission. We present a new non-parametric foreground-cleaning method in visibility space for low-redshift HI intensity mapping and apply it to interferometric MeerKAT and SKA-Mid-like simulations. Our newly-developed Generalised Visibility Internal Linear Combination (GVILC) method combines Principal Component Analysis (PCA) for foreground subspace identification, informed by a prior HI signal-plus-noise covariance matrix, with a multi-dimensional ILC in visibility space to suppress foregrounds and recover the HI signal with reduced contamination. The number of modes to remove in the PCA is calculated using a data-driven statistical approach, leading to significant foreground residual suppression with limited HI signal loss. Moreover, GVILC in combination with foreground avoidance can prevent most of the signal loss, while simultaneously recovering modes that were initially contaminated by foregrounds. The mean squared error statistic shows that for both MeerKAT and SKA-Mid-like simulations, GVILC outperforms the foreground avoidance only strategy. We find that including a more realistic HI model with line broadening and spatial correlations between HI and continuum sources leads to increased signal loss after foreground removal, without affecting the number of modes removed by GVILC. This paper demonstrates that GVILC is an effective method to clean foreground contamination in HI intensity mapping with radio interferometric data, enabling the recovery of cosmological information from previously inaccessible regions of Fourier space.

</details>


### [122] [A Global Overview of Starobinsky Inflation beyond slow-roll with CMB-BAO data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19296)
*Tarun,Tanmoy Modak,Björn Malte Schäfer*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文利用ACT、SPT最新CMB数据及DESI BAO测量，对Starobinsky暴胀模型进行全局分析，通过直接求解运动方程获得比慢滚近似下更严格的参数约束，并进一步推导出对再热温度、R³修正项及HSR/PSR参数化等的限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 改进Starobinsky暴胀模型的参数约束精度，超越传统慢滚近似，并探讨其与再热物理、BAO参数及高阶修正模型的关联。

Method: 基于ACT、SPT和DESI BAO最新观测数据，直接数值求解暴胀场运动方程，结合贝叶斯参数估计进行全局拟合；分析先验选择影响及参数与rdh的相关性；扩展至R³修正模型及HSR/PSR参数化框架。

Result: 获得比慢滚近似更紧致的原始功率谱振幅As与标量谱指数ns约束；导出再热温度上限；揭示参数对rdh的显著相关性；给出R³模型新参数及HSR/PSR参数的有效限制。

Conclusion: 直接求解运动方程显著提升Starobinsky模型参数限制能力；再热物理与BAO数据对暴胀模型甄别具有关键作用；R³修正及慢滚参数化均受当前数据有效约束。

Abstract: We perform a global analysis of the Starobinsky inflation using most recent CMB data from ACT and SPT together with DESI BAO measurements. By directly solving the equations of motion, we find relatively stronger constraints on the model parameters than those obtained under the slow-roll approximation from the baseline $Λ$CDM parameters $A_s$ and $n_s$. We translate these constraints into limits on the reheating temperature. We also discuss the dependence of the prior choices of the model parameters. Further, the correlation of the model parameters with the BAO parameter $r_d h$ is also studied. We further investigate the impact of the global analysis on the dimension-six $R^3$ modified Starobinsky model, deriving constraints on its additional parameters. Finally, we also provide parameter limits on the effective inflationary Hubble slow-roll (HSR) and potential slow-roll (PSR) parametrization.

</details>


### [123] [Monitoring the power spectrum of magnetic field fluctuations across the simulated intracluster medium](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19307)
*T. Bartalesi,F. Vazza,S. Ettori,C. Nipoti,P. Dominguez-Fernandez*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文通过高精度磁流体动力学模拟，研究了后并合星系团中磁场功率谱随ICM环境和动力学特性的变化规律，并提出一种扩展模型（包含小尺度发电机效应和Kolmogorov型成分）来更好拟合不同区域的磁场功率谱，发现拟合参数与局部ICM密度和团块性显著相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用当前及未来射电干涉仪观测到的法拉第旋转测量图，结合磁场功率谱模型，严格约束星系团内介质（ICM）磁场性质；特别关注后并合星系团中磁场功率谱如何随ICM环境和动力学状态变化。

Method: 基于非辐射、宇宙学尺度的磁流体动力学模拟，将模拟星系团划分为边长950 kpc的立方子区域，分别计算各子区域动能与磁场功率谱；在已有‘仅发电机’模型基础上引入Kolmogorov型成分构建扩展模型，并用马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛方法对每个子区域独立拟合；分析拟合参数与对应子区域ICM局地性质（如密度、团块性）的相关性。

Result: 扩展模型在大多数子区域能较好复现磁场功率谱数据，而‘仅发电机’模型仅在近中心区域拟合良好；拟合参数在子区域间存在显著差异，且与局部ICM密度和团块性呈强相关。

Conclusion: 磁场功率谱与局部ICM环境密切相关，该结果有助于观测者更准确地解读法拉第旋转观测数据，并为理解ICM磁场起源与演化提供理论支持。

Abstract: Thanks to the current and forthcoming radio interferometers, Faraday rotation measure maps extend out to the outskirts of galaxy clusters. Interpreted through models of the magnetic power spectrum, these maps can provide stringent constraints on the properties of the magnetic field of the intracluster medium (ICM). Our goal is to ascertain how the magnetic power spectrum varies with the environment and dynamics of the ICM in a post-merger galaxy cluster formed in a state-of-the-art non-radiative cosmological magneto-hydrodynamic simulation. We divide the simulated cluster into cubic subboxes with side length 950 kpc and compute the kinetic and magnetic power spectra in each. Starting from a "dynamo-only" functional form proposed in previous works to describe magnetic fluctuations amplified by the small-scale dynamo, we introduce an additional Kolmogorov-like component to account for the contribution of unamplified magnetic field patches. We fit this extended model to the magnetic power spectrum of each subbox individually using a Markov chain Monte Carlo method and examine scatter plots of the inferred parameters versus the local ICM properties evaluated in the corresponding subboxes. The extended model reproduces the magnetic power spectrum data well in most subboxes, whereas the "dynamo-only" model fits these data well only in subboxes not too far from the center. The inferred parameters exhibit substantial subbox-to-subbox variations that correlate significantly with the local ICM density and clumpiness. These correlations suggest that the magnetic power spectrum is closely linked to the local ICM environment, and may help observers in the interpretation of Faraday Rotation data.

</details>


### [124] [Distinguishing Coupled Dark Matter Dark Energy from Kinematic Phantom Crossing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19602)
*Samit Ganguly,Koushik Dutta,Goutam Manna*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究了耦合暗能量-暗物质（CDEDM）模型中能量交换如何产生类似幽灵场的物态方程演化（w < -1），并揭示其在结构增长中的可观测特征，包括与中微子质量的简并、第五力与拖曳效应的竞争、以及暗物质质量随时间演化的效应，从而可区分动力学相互作用与纯运动学暗能量参数化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期宇宙膨胀观测显示暗能量物态方程可能穿越w = -1，引发对‘幽灵型’行为的关注；但该现象未必需引入根本性幽灵场，而可能源于标准标量场与冷暗物质间的能量交换，因此需探究其微观机制与可观测后果。

Method: 构建耦合暗能量-暗物质（CDEDM）模型，其中标量场与冷暗物质通过拉格朗日标签交换能量；建立两流体（CDM+重子）增长方程，纳入标量介导的第五力与拖曳项，并耦合自由流中微子；结合DES DR2 BAO、ACT DR6透镜、CMB距离、RSD及三组超新星数据进行联合拟合；解析推导中微子质量与耦合强度的简并关系。

Result: 发现耦合诱导的结构增长具有独特印记：① 生成∑m_ν ∝ f_c²α² 的解析简并关系；② 增长响应主要源于背景演化修正，第五力促进增长、拖曳部分抵消；③ 暗物质质量在再复合时期变化4.3%–5.5%，要求CMB距离计算计入其演化；④ 指数势与逆幂律势均复现该缩放律。

Conclusion: CDEDM模型能自然再现w < -1演化，且其在结构形成与再复合时期的协同效应（增长特征+暗物质质量演化）构成区别于纯参数化w₀wₐCDM模型的关键观测判据，为检验暗能量本质提供了新途径。

Abstract: Recent measurements of cosmic expansion have renewed interest in dark-energy scenarios in which the effective equation of state exhibits phantom-like evolution and may cross $w=-1$. Such behavior, however, does not require a fundamental phantom field: in coupled dark-energy-dark-matter~(CDEDM) models, energy exchange between a canonical scalar field and cold dark matter, happens Lagrangian labels, can reproduce the same effective equation of state evolution as that of phenomenological $w_0w_a$CDM model. We show that this microscopic interaction leaves distinctive, testable signatures in structure growth by forming a two-fluid system in which only cold dark matter feels the scalar-mediated fifth force (and an associated drag term), while baryons remain uncoupled. When combined with neutrino-free streaming, this setup generates a characteristic coupling, neutrino-mass degeneracy, whose leading scaling $\sum m_ν\propto f_c^2 α^2$ follows analytically from the two-fluid growth equations. Using DESI DR2 BAO, CMB distance information, ACT DR6 lensing, redshift-space distortions, and three supernova compilations, we find that this scaling is recovered for both exponential and inverse-power-law potentials. We further show that the dominant contribution to the growth response arises from the coupling-induced modification of the background evolution, with the fifth force enhancing growth and the drag partially counteracting it. Finally, the interaction induces a running dark-matter mass, changing it at recombination by $4.3\%$--$5.5\%$, so CMB distance calculations must account for the evolving dark-matter density. These correlated growth and recombination effects provide a route to distinguish interaction-driven phantom crossing from purely kinematic dark-energy parametrizations.

</details>


### [125] [Probing submillimeter number counts below the confusion limit: extreme-value statistics of the P(D) distribution and its modulation by gravitational lensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19689)
*Kaustuv Basu,Andrea Guerrero,Frank Bertoldi*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于极值理论（峰值超阈值法）的新方法来分析亚毫米星系的混淆P(D)分布尾部，利用广义帕累托分布（GPD）的形状参数ξ(u)直接、归一化无关地读取星系数目函数的局部对数斜率，并通过其引力透镜调制Δξ(u)探测其局部曲率。该方法经模拟验证，并应用于Planck和Herschel/SPIRE数据，首次在P(D)尾部中探测到透镜效应，支持Schechter模型，并为未来高分辨率巡天提供了新统计工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统P(D)分析压缩信息、依赖完整仪器前向模型；而亚毫米星系数目函数在混淆极限以下难以直接观测，亟需一种鲁棒、归一化无关、能分辨函数形式并探测透镜效应的新统计方法。

Method: 引入极值理论中的峰值超阈值（POT）方法，将P(D)尾部建模为广义帕累托分布（GPD）；推导ξ(u)与数目函数局部对数斜率及Δξ(u)与透镜调制曲率的解析关系；通过端到端模拟验证，并应用于Planck 857 GHz与Herschel/SPIRE 350 μm实测图。

Result: 在SPIRE分辨率下，ξ(u)随阈值显著上升，首次在P(D)尾部中探测到强透镜效应；亮源掩模后数据支持Schechter模型；在星系团后方给出Δξ(u)首个校准上限；Planck数据因分辨率低主要反映亮源而非本征 faint counts。

Conclusion: GPD尾部统计量ξ(u)是一种鲁棒、归一化无关、分辨率自适应的工具，可在探测极限以下分辨星系数目函数形态；引力透镜提供校准‘标尺’以刻画数目函数特征；其可靠探测有待深场、高分辨率、大质量星系团巡天。

Abstract: The shape of the submillimeter galaxy number counts below the confusion limit is a key record of cosmic star formation but is accessible only statistically, through the one-point distribution of map surface brightness, $P(D)$. Classical $P(D)$ analysis compresses the counts into flux-integrated constraints and requires a full instrument forward model. We introduce an extreme-value-theory analysis of the confusion $P(D)$ tail: the peaks-over-threshold formalism, in which exceedances above a threshold $u$ follow a generalized Pareto distribution (GPD). The GPD shape parameter $ξ(u)$ is a flux-resolved, normalization-free readout of the local logarithmic slope of the counts, and its gravitational-lensing modulation $Δξ(u)$ probes their local curvature. We derive analytic relations for both, validate them with end-to-end simulations, and apply the method to Planck 857 GHz maps and the Herschel/SPIRE 350 $μ$m map of GAMA-09. At Planck's 5' resolution the tail reflects the bright, clustered sky rather than the faint counts, though the background alone excludes the single power-law count model. At SPIRE resolution $ξ$ rises markedly with threshold, consistent with the strongly lensed bright population (a first detection of lensing in a $P(D)$ tail), and the bright-masked map favors the Schechter model. Behind galaxy clusters we set the first calibrated upper limits on $Δξ(u)$. CCAT/FYST should separate the count models directly, but a cluster-lensing detection needs more $10^{15}\,M_\odot$ clusters than the sky contains. The GPD tail statistic thus discriminates the functional form of the counts at fluxes of order the threshold, below the detection limit, invariant to map mean, gain and count normalization, and robust to clustering; lensing supplies a calibrated ruler for count features, whose detection awaits deep, high-resolution surveys of massive clusters.

</details>


### [126] [Constraints on the Pop III Sky Surface Brightness from High-Redshift Caustic Transients in MACS0416](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19757)
*Jakob P. Perivolotis,Pietro Bergamini,Rogier A. Windhorst,Erik Zackrisson,Timothy Carleton,Seth H. Cohen,Jordan C. J. D'Silva,Rachel Honor,Rolf A. Jansen,Anton M. Koekemoer,Rafael Ortiz,Jake Summers,Dan Coe,Christopher J. Conselice,Jose M. Diego,Brenda Frye,Massimo Ricotti,Gibson B. Bowling,Brent Smith,Christopher N. A. Willmer,Haojing Yan*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文通过分析MACS J0416.1-2403星系团的多期观测数据，搜寻高红移（z=7–17）原初恒星（Pop III）在引力透镜临界曲线附近的短暂增亮事件，未发现新事例；由此设定了z≥7未分辨Pop III恒星群体更严格的表面亮度上限，并对Pop III恒星的临界曲线穿越率给出了统计约束（95%上限为0.86 cluster⁻¹ yr⁻¹），为未来观测提供基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Pop III恒星因极低光度难以被JWST直接探测，但借助星系团强引力透镜在临界曲线附近的极端放大效应，或可捕捉其短暂增亮信号；本文旨在利用多期观测提升探测灵敏度并给出实证约束。

Method: 基于强引力透镜模型构建z=7–17源的临界曲线，对MACS J0416.1-2403三次跨度126天的观测数据作差分成像，人工检查候选体；结合模型中更长临界曲线带来的更大源面扫过面积，提升统计灵敏度，并将零结果建模为泊松过程以推断穿越率。

Result: 未发现新的高红移caustic transit事件；得到100倍太阳质量Pop III恒星在2微米波段的表面亮度下限为32.8±0.6 mag arcsec⁻²；推得caustic-transit率后验均值为0.29 cluster⁻¹ yr⁻¹，95%上限为λ₉₅ = 0.86 cluster⁻¹ yr⁻¹。

Conclusion: 当前观测未支持高丰度明亮Pop III恒星的存在，所得约束与标准模型一致，验证了利用多期透镜巡天限制早期宇宙恒星种群可行，为后续类似项目提供了关键实证基准。

Abstract: Population III (Pop III) stars are hypothetical zero-metallicity stellar structures formed from primordial hydrogen and helium. They are theorized to span a wide mass range, extending to several 100 solar masses, and may have played important roles in nucleosynthesis and reionization. Their expected fluxes are far below JWST NIRCam detection limits, making direct observation unlikely. However, extreme magnification near the caustics of massive foreground galaxy clusters may enable their detection. We search for overlooked high-redshift (7 <= z <= 17) caustic transits in the lensing cluster MACS J0416.1-2403. Using three observations spanning 126 days, we create difference images to identify potential candidates. Critical curves for sources at 7 <= z <= 17, derived from a strong-lensing model, guided visual inspection of three difference-images. No additional transits were found. The longer caustics in our model sweep a larger source-plane area, increasing the probability of detecting an event. Combining this increased statistical sensitivity with deeper imaging, we establish a fainter limit for the unresolved stellar population at z >= 7. From this null result, we constrain the 100 solar mass 2 micron Pop III sky surface brightness to >= 32.8 +/- 0.6 mag arcsec^-2. Modeling the non-detection as a Poisson process gives a posterior mean caustic-transit rate of 0.29 cluster^-1 yr^-1 and a 95 percent upper credible limit of lambda_95 = 0.86 cluster^-1 yr^-1. The inferred rate remains consistent with the adopted fiducial Pop III caustic-transit model and provides an empirical benchmark for future multi-epoch monitoring campaigns. This search exploits the fact that individual stars projected close to source-plane caustics can be briefly magnified far beyond their unlensed fluxes. These constraints provide a direct test of the abundance of luminous Pop III stars at early cosmic times.

</details>


### [127] [Predictions on the abundance of primordial black holes: results from the SMILE VLBI milli-lensing sample](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20329)

TL;DR: 本文利用SMILE样本中的约5000个平谱致密射电源，通过甚长基线干涉测量（VLBI）对中高质量范围（10^6–10^9 M⊙）的原初黑洞（PBH）暗物质丰度进行预测，在无确认微引力透镜事件的前提下，给出f_PBH ≲ 0.12% 的95%上限，比以往VLBI限制紧约一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中高质量范围（10^6–10^9 M⊙）的原初黑洞（PBH）作为暗物质候选体，其丰度目前约束较弱；而该质量范围对应的爱因斯坦半径在毫角秒量级，VLBI是探测其微透镜效应最直接的手段。

Method: 基于SMILE大样本（~5000个平谱致密射电源），结合VLBI观测参数（1.5 mas分辨率、100 mas最大像分离、40倍最大可探测流量比），计算微透镜光学深度，并考虑放大偏差、有限核分辨性及吸积导致的质量增长等修正因素，推导PBH暗物质丰度f_PBH的上限。

Result: 在乐观观测条件下且未发现微透镜事件时，得到f_PBH ≲ 0.12%（95%置信度）的上限，比此前VLBI结果强约10倍；若存在1个确证事件，上限放宽至0.20%；考虑对数正态质量函数、放大偏差与有限核分辨性仅使限制变化约5%；吸积导致的质量增长影响小于1%。

Conclusion: VLBI对SMILE样本的微透镜搜寻有望显著提升中高质量PBH暗物质的限制精度；结果高度依赖于候选事件的可靠确认，凸显后续证认工作的重要性；整体结论对多种系统性效应具有鲁棒性。

Abstract: Primordial black holes (PBHs) formed in the early universe represent a well-motivated dark matter candidate. However, their abundance in the intermediate-to-high mass range ($10^6$-$10^9\,M_\odot$) remains relatively less constrained. In this mass regime, the corresponding Einstein radii on the milliarcsecond scale make Very Long Baseline Interferometry (VLBI) the most direct observational probe via milli-lensing of compact radio sources. In this paper, we present predictions for the abundance of PBH dark matter ($f_{\rm PBH}$) using the large SMILE sample of $\sim 5000$ flat-spectrum compact radio sources. Under the optimistic observational conditions (with an angular resolution of $1.5\,\mathrm{mas}$, a maximum image separation of $100\,\mathrm{mas}$, and a maximum detectable flux ratio of $40$), assuming that no milli-lensing events are confirmed, we obtain the projected 95% upper limit of $f_{\rm PBH}\lesssim0.12%$ in the intermediate-to-high mass range, which would be approximately an order of magnitude tighter than previous VLBI milli-lensing limits. Broader lognormal mass functions yield correspondingly weaker projected limits. Generalising the optical depth to account for magnification bias and finite-core resolvability changes these projected limits by only about $5$%. Moreover, we show that the predicted limits are highly sensitive to a small number of confirmed events, i.e., even one confirmed milli-lensing event would relax the projected upper limit to $0.20$%, highlighting the critical importance of robust candidate confirmation in the SMILE sample. Finally, under the optimistic baryonic accretion scenario, the PBH mass growth changes the projected limits by $\lesssim 1$% within the VLBI-sensitive mass range, confirming the robustness of our results.

TL;DR: 本文通过盲挑战评估了九种基于弱引力透镜效应的星系团探测算法在Euclid类模拟数据上的性能，筛选出四种互补性方法（AMICO-WL、DoG、O21、W234），组合后显著提升探测完整性（达70%以上）和纯度，预计Euclid DR1将据此发现约2500个星系团。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为迎接欧几里得（Euclid）巡天即将发布的海量弱引力透镜数据，亟需系统评估并优化适用于其高密度背景星系图像的星系团探测算法。

Method: 在DEMNUni-Cov模拟的1200 deg² Euclid类弱透镜观测数据上开展盲挑战，评估九种算法；采用信噪比>2、z-M选择函数及两种匹配策略进行性能评测；从中预选出四种原理互补的方法（最优滤波、高斯滤波、孔径质量滤波、多尺度小波滤波）并研究其组合效果。

Result: 单个最优算法在平均纯度90%下仅达约10%完整性；四种方法组合后对低红移、高质星星系团的完整性提升至70%以上；外推至Euclid第一数据发布（DR1），预计可探测约2500个星系团。

Conclusion: 多算法融合是提升Euclid弱透镜星系团探测性能的关键路径，该盲挑战为后续真实数据处理提供了经验证的稳健方法框架。

Abstract: Weak gravitational lensing offers a powerful way to detect galaxy clusters by directly tracing their total matter content. Its effectiveness increases with the density of background galaxies, a requirement that is now being met by the high sensitivity and wide coverage of current large-area surveys. A prime example is the Euclid telescope, which will map approximately 14 000 deg2 of the sky, measuring the shapes of billions of galaxies and opening a meaningful window for detecting galaxy clusters uniquely through their weak lensing signal. We present the results of nine galaxy cluster detection algorithms in a blind challenge, using 1200 deg2 of Euclid-like weak lensing observations from the DEMNUni-Cov simulations. The performance of these methods was assessed by matching their detections to known synthetic clusters with signal-to-noise ratios greater than 2, satisfying the z-M selection cut, and adopting two different matching procedures. The purpose of the challenge was to identify and improve strategies for galaxy cluster detection via weak lensing for the upcoming Euclid data releases. We pre-selected four methods based on their individual performance and complementarity. Each pre-selected method adopts a distinct approach: AMICO-WL uses an optimal filtering technique, DoG employs Gaussian filtering, O21 relies on aperture-mass filtering, and W234 applies multi-scale wavelet filtering. Together, the results of these methods can be merged to enhance the processing of Euclid data. Individually, these algorithms reach approximately 10% completeness for a mean purity of 90%, while their combination leads to roughly a two-fold improvement in overall performance, exceeding 70% completeness for low-redshift, high-mass clusters. When extrapolating the results of this work to Euclid Data Release 1, we expect to detect approximately 2500 galaxy clusters via weak gravitational lensing.

### [129] [Gravitas: A Browser-Based Astrophysics Laboratory for Prediction, Measurement, and Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19327)
*Carl Ziegler*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: Gravitas是一个免费的、基于浏览器的天体物理模拟环境，旨在通过可配置的引力沙盒和大量引导式探究活动，帮助天文与物理初学者在无法实验的系统中开展预测-检验-测量-修正-解释的科学探究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 天文学学生常需推理无法实验室操作、演化时间尺度过长或仅能间接观测的系统；现有教学工具难以支持此类探究式学习。

Method: 开发了Gravitas 1.0.0版本——一个集成引力沙盒、59个即用场景、22个含636步的引导探究活动、多模态观测显示（凌星、视向速度等）及配套教学支持功能（教师指南、本地化、无障碍访问、离线运行等）的交互式平台。

Result: 实现了支持预测-检验-测量-修正-解释循环的学习体验，提供可追溯的证据记录与报告导出功能，并通过公共仪表盘公开243项科学校验结果及其依据与容差。

Conclusion: Gravitas并非替代定量计算或真实观测，而是为初学者提供一个以实证为基础构建物理解释的低门槛、高参与度的探究空间，适用于入门级天文与物理课程。

Abstract: Astronomy students are often asked to reason about systems that cannot be handled in a laboratory, evolve over inaccessible timescales, or reveal themselves only through indirect measurements. Gravitas is a free, browser-based astrophysics environment designed to turn those limitations into opportunities for experimentation. Its version 1.0.0 release combines a configurable gravitational sandbox with 59 ready-to-run scenarios and 22 guided investigations comprising 636 steps and roughly 15-19 hours of structured work. Students can build and alter systems, change reference frames, measure distances, angles, periods, energies, forces, and orbital elements, and observe simulated systems through transit, radial-velocity, astrometric, rotation-curve, stellar-evolution, and gravitational-wave displays. Guided activities use a predict-test-measure-revise-explain cycle, retain the student's evidence, and export a report for submission. Instructor guides, answer keys, short classroom routes, lecture and embed modes, reproducible share links, Spanish localization, keyboard-accessible navigation, and offline operation lower the practical barrier to adopting the simulation in introductory astronomy and physics courses. A public dashboard reports 243 scientific checks across 25 areas and names the sources and tolerances behind them. This article describes the learning experiences available in Gravitas, illustrates several ways it can be used from a brief demonstration to a full laboratory exercise, and states the scientific scope and limitations of the models. The aim is not to replace quantitative calculation or observation, but to give students a place where they can make a prediction, change one thing, measure what follows, and build an explanation from their own evidence.

</details>

*Yukiho Kobayashi,Dai Tateishi,Yuji Sunada,Takuto Kiyomoto,Nobuaki Sasaki,Kazuki Abe,Franca Cassol,Shuichi Gunji,Daniela Hadasch,Dirk Hoffmann,Julien Houles,Yusuke Inome,Hideaki Katagiri,Hidetoshi Kubo,Junko Kushida,Daniel Mazin,Masaya Mizote,Tsutomu Nagayoshi,Takeshi Nakamori,Kyoshi Nishijima,Seiya Nozaki,Hideyuki Ohoka,Akira Okumura,Reiko Orito,Takayuki Saito,Shunsuke Sakurai,Mitsunari Takahashi,Yukikatsu Terada,Masahiro Teshima,Tokonatsu Yamamoto,Tatsuo Yoshida*

TL;DR: 本文研究了成像大气切伦科夫望远镜中光电倍增管（PMT）增益随地磁场方向变化的现象，通过现场校准数据和实验室测量，确认并量化了地磁场对PMT增益及过剩噪声因子的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CTAO LST-1校准期间发现PMT响应随望远镜指向（即相对地磁场方向）变化，需明确该现象是否源于地磁场效应。

Method: 分析LST-1现场校准数据，并开展专用实验室测量，分别评估地磁场对PMT增益和过剩噪声因子的影响。

Result: 现场与实验室测量均一致显示PMT增益受地磁场影响；实验室结果进一步区分了其对增益和过剩噪声因子的独立影响。

Conclusion: LST-1观测中观测到的PMT增益变化可明确归因于地磁场效应，为后续校准和数据分析提供了物理依据。

Abstract: Photomultiplier tubes (PMTs) are widely used in imaging atmospheric Cherenkov telescopes. Their response can depend on the telescope pointing direction through changes in their orientation relative to the geomagnetic field. Such a dependence was indicated during the calibration campaign of the CTAO LST-1. In this work, the LST-1 calibration data are analyzed to quantify the gain dependence in terms of the geomagnetic field, and dedicated laboratory measurements are performed to test the hypothesis that the observed dependence originates from magnetic-field effects. Both the on-site and laboratory measurements show a consistent dependence of the PMT gain on the magnetic field, while the laboratory measurements further separate the effects on the gain and the excess noise factor. These results provide a clearer understanding of the gain variation observed in LST-1 in terms of magnetic-field effects.

</details>

*Aaron J. Crepp,Brian L. Sands,Justin R. Crepp*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于衍射极限径向速度光谱仪的半自动单模光纤（SMF）耦合控制程序，显著提升了星像与SMF的对准精度和耦合效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未来高精度径向速度测量仪器将越来越多地采用自适应光学和单模光纤，但将星光高效、稳定地耦合进仅5-10微米直径的近红外单模光纤技术难度大，亟需高效稳定的对准方法。

Method: 开发了一种半自动单模光纤耦合控制程序，并通过代表性仿真验证其性能。

Result: 控制器可将星像-光纤间距从约3.9倍模场直径（MFD）减小至0.11 MFD，归一化耦合效率从零提升至0.6–0.8；同时研究了其在近距双星场景中的适用性。

Conclusion: 该半自动耦合控制程序能显著提高单模光纤耦合效率与稳定性，适用于下一代高精度衍射极限径向速度光谱仪。

Abstract: Conventional astronomical spectrographs are seeing-limited and commonly use multimode fibers to inject starlight into downstream optics. Forthcoming Doppler radial-velocity (RV) instruments are expected to make increasing use of adaptive optics and smaller single-mode fibers (SMF), enabling higher spectral resolution and improved stabilization of the spectrograph point-spread function. However, coupling starlight into SMFs with characteristic diameters of only 5-10 microns at near-infrared wavelengths is technically demanding, requiring alignment procedures that are both efficient and stable throughout the duration of an observation. In this paper, we describe a semi-automated SMF coupling control program being developed for diffraction-limited RV spectrographs. Representative simulations demonstrate that the controller reduces the star-fiber separation from approximately 3.9 mode field diameters (MFD) to 0.11 MFD while increasing a normalized coupling metric from zero to values near 0.6-0.8 after acquisition. Application to closely-separated binary stars is also studied.

</details>


### [132] [The SHARPEx space-to-space VLBI experiment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20102)
*Freek Roelofs,Christiaan Brinkerink,Albert Catalan Artigas,Heino Falcke,Jaime Fernández,Christian M. Fromm,Felix Glaser,Marc Klein Wolt,Yuri Kovalev,Volodymyr Kudriashov,María Manzano,Rasmus Maråk,Manuel Martin-Neira,Jacobo Pastor Fernández-Posse López,Montserrat Puertolas Turell,Alberto Rodríguez Pérez-Silva,René Rüddenklau,Efthalia Traianou,Roger Vilaseca Miro*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文提出了SHARPEx任务概念，旨在通过两颗CubeSat或SmallSat卫星实现空间VLBI关键技术验证，为未来亚毫米波空间阵列（如SHARP）成像黑洞光子环、测量黑洞自旋和时空结构奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地面事件视界望远镜受限于地球尺寸和大气影响，难以在高频率下显著提升分辨率；空间VLBI是突破该瓶颈、实现超高清黑洞成像的必由之路。

Method: 提出SHARPEx任务概念，采用两颗搭载小型天线（~1米）的立方星/微小卫星，在厘米波段开展技术验证，重点突破星间时钟同步、星上相关处理和亚波长相对轨道测定等空间VLBI核心使能技术。

Result: SHARPEx作为技术验证任务，将首次在轨演示空间VLBI关键能力；其后续增强型任务有望以 unprecedented 分辨率和保真度对明亮活动星系核（AGN）成像。

Conclusion: SHARPEx是迈向空间亚毫米波VLBI成像黑洞光子环与测量广义相对论效应的关键一步，验证了基于小型卫星平台实现高精度空间干涉测量的可行性。

Abstract: Space is the next frontier for ultra-high resolution imaging of astrophysical sources with very long baseline interferometry (VLBI). The limited size of the Earth and atmospheric absorption and turbulence at high frequencies prevent order-of-magnitude resolution improvements from current black hole imaging with the ground-based Event Horizon Telescope. A two or three-element submm space-based array like SHARP would image the thin black hole photon ring and measure the black hole spin and spacetime. Here, we present the SHARP Experiment (SHARPEx) mission concept. SHARPEx will demonstrate key enabling technologies for space-to-space VLBI, including clock syntonization between two satellites, on-board correlation, and sub-wavelength relative orbit determination. SHARPEx will consist of two CubeSat or SmallSat platforms carrying small (~1 m diameter) antennas, observing at cm wavelengths. While SHARPEx will be a technical demonstrator, a SHARPEx-type follow-up mission with enhanced intersatellite link properties (range and bandwidth) may produce images of bright AGN with unprecedented resolution and fidelity.

</details>


### [133] [Offline vs Real-Time Validation and Performance Assessment of Heimdall Detection Pipeline for Fast Radio Bursts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20635)
*Valentina Cesare,Giovanni Naldi,Francesco Fiori,Andrea Geminardi,Adrian De Barro,Alessio Magro,Hayley Camilleri*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文探讨了在射电天文数据处理中，从离线模式向实时模式转变的必要性，并以Heimdall脉冲检测流水线为实验对象，在PSRDADA（实时）和SIGPROC（离线）两种模式下进行对比测试，验证其在精度（TOA、DM误差<3%，SNR误差<10%）和实时性方面的可行性，为后续集成波束成形器奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前及未来射电望远镜（如SKA）产生的PB级/年数据量使传统离线I/O密集型处理流水线难以为继，亟需转向基于内存的实时处理范式（如PSRDADA环形缓冲区）。

Method: 采用开源Heimdall检测流水线，支持单GPU上的非相干消色散处理；在Northern Cross望远镜NS臂16个柱面采集的数据上，分别运行PSRDADA（实时）与SIGPROC（离线）两种输入模式，并系统比较FRB关键参数（到达时间TOA、色散量DM、信噪比SNR）及执行时间。

Result: PSRDADA与SIGPROC结果在TOA和DM上偏差<3%，SNR偏差<10%；含真实FRB的数据多数情况下PSRDADA模式满足实时性要求，整体执行时间与SIGPROC模式相当。

Conclusion: 该实验证明Heimdall在PSRDADA实时框架下具备足够精度与实时性能，是迈向集成波束成形器+Heimdall实时流水线的关键预备步骤。

Abstract: Data rates from current radio telescopes are of $\sim$O(10)PB/y and we expect a growth of an order of magnitude for future radio telescopes, e.g. SKA. Coping with these data flows requires a paradigm change in data management: building pipelines with intense I/O between their modules (offline mode) is becoming unsustainable, leading to a transition to a real-time paradigm, where data are moved to RAM, e.g.,through PSRDADA ring buffers. Our objective is to obtain a compact pipeline to efficiently manage data in the real-time flow, while minimizing HPC resources. We employ Heimdall, an open source detection pipeline that performs incoherent de-dispersion, to detect single pulses in radio astronomy data, such as FRBs, astrophysical signals whose origin has not been completely explained. Heimdall currently processes a single beam on a single GPU and can operate both in offline and real-time modes, reading in input either a SIGPROC filterbank file or a PSRDADA ring buffer. We run a test pipeline with data collected from 16 cylinders of the NS arm of the Northern Cross radio telescope near Medicina, both in offline and real-time modes, to compare the obtained results and performance. To corroborate these tests, we employ different input data and explore the problem's parameter space. PSRDADA and SIGPROC results generally agree within 3% for the most important quantities characterizing a FRB (TOA and DM) and within 10% for the SNR. Execution times in PSRDADA mode stay in the real-time flow for most runs from data containing true FRBs but they are still generally comparable with SIGPROC mode in this testbed setup. This test provides an important preliminary comparison in terms of accuracy and performance: this work is functional and preparatory for the next integration of the beamformer with the Heimdall pipeline, having the same logical structure as the test pipeline, work already in progress.

</details>


### [134] [All-Fiber Broadband Wavelength-Division Multiplexing using Heterogeneous Photonic Lanterns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20642)
*Abani Shankar Nayak,Julius Goehring,Martin M. Roth,Kalaga Madhav*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于1×7异质光子灯笼（PL）的全光纤宽带波分复用架构，通过组合不同单模光纤（SM450和SMF28）核心，在可见光与H波段分别实现宽带集光与被动波长选择性空间路由。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为满足下一代天文观测设施对多波长同步观测及馈送波段优化光谱仪的需求，需发展兼具宽带响应与波长选择能力的新型光纤器件。

Method: 设计并制备两种1×7异质光子灯笼（PL-(6+1) 和 PL-(4+3)），利用无严格几何约束的非匹配单模光纤芯（SM450与SMF28）构成自由封装光纤束；实验表征其在350–740 nm（可见光）与1520–1610 nm（H波段）的传输特性。

Result: 在可见光波段，器件表现为多通道宽带集光器，各通道均有效导光且无空间分离；在H波段，因芯不对称性解除模式简并、优化传播常数匹配，实现被动光谱分选；验证了自由封装异质光纤束可满足绝热过渡条件。

Conclusion: 该异质光子灯笼架构无需主动调控即可实现双波段差异化功能，为天文多波段光纤馈送系统提供了紧凑、高效、全光纤的新方案。

Abstract: This work presents an all-fiber broadband wavelength-division multiplexing architecture using 1x7 heterogeneous photonic lanterns (PLs), specifically PL-(6+1) and PL-(4+3). By combining mismatched single-mode cores, namely SM450 and SMF28, without rigid geometric constraints, these devices break system degeneracy and enable passive, wavelength-selective spatial routing. Experimental characterization reveals distinct dual-regime performance across a wide spectral range. In the visible band (350-740 nm), the devices function as multi-channel broadband collectors, effectively guiding light across all channels without spatial separation. Conversely, in the H-band (1520-1610 nm), core asymmetries lift mode degeneracy and maximize propagation constant (beta) matching conditions, thereby enabling passive spectral sorting. These results confirm that unconstrained, free-packaged heterogeneous fiber bundles can meet adiabatic transition criteria, showcasing their potential for next-generation astronomical facilities that require simultaneous multi-wavelength capabilities to feed separate, band-optimized spectrographs.

</details>
